Ferros Abda Sayd had considerablc difficulty in rendednt his translation. This
rras parrly due to thc ncccssity of dealing with notions, ju_ridical institutions and
rerminologt which rlcre unfamiliar in Ethiopia. ln additiorl Guidi notes, thc Arabic
wrsion the translator had appears to harc lacled vorcls and sorne diacritical marks
